local function _b64dec(data)
    local b = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
    local t = {}
    for i = 1, 64 do
        t[string.byte(b, i)] = i - 1
    end
    t[string.byte("=")] = 0

    local out = {}
    local out_count = 0
    local chunks = {}
    local len = #data
    local i = 1

    while i <= len do
        local c1 = t[string.byte(data, i)] or 0
        local c2 = t[string.byte(data, i + 1)] or 0
        local c3 = t[string.byte(data, i + 2)] or 0
        local c4 = t[string.byte(data, i + 3)] or 0

        local b1 = (c1 * 4) + math.floor(c2 / 16)
        local b2 = ((c2 % 16) * 16) + math.floor(c3 / 4)
        local b3 = ((c3 % 4) * 64) + c4

        out_count = out_count + 1
        out[out_count] = string.char(b1)
        if string.byte(data, i + 2) ~= 61 then
            out_count = out_count + 1
            out[out_count] = string.char(b2)
        end
        if string.byte(data, i + 3) ~= 61 then
            out_count = out_count + 1
            out[out_count] = string.char(b3)
        end
        i = i + 4
        if out_count > 4096 then
            table.insert(chunks, table.concat(out))
            out = {}
            out_count = 0
        end
    end
    if out_count > 0 then
        table.insert(chunks, table.concat(out))
    end
    return table.concat(chunks)
end

local function _D()
    local b = _b64dec(table.concat(_P, ""))
    local l = string.len(b)
    local kl = string.len(_K)
    local o = {}
    local c = 0
    local c_t = {}
    for i = 1, l do
        local e = string.byte(b, i)
        local k = string.byte(_K, ((i - 1) % kl) + 1)
        local f = (((i - 1) * 7 + 13) % 256)
        local d = (e - k - f) % 256
        c = c + 1
        o[c] = string.char(d)
        if c > 4096 then
            table.insert(c_t, table.concat(o))
            o = {}
            c = 0
        end
    end
    if c > 0 then
        table.insert(c_t, table.concat(o))
    end
    return table.concat(c_t)
end

local _E = _D()
local _F, _err = load(_E, "cps_protected")
if _F then
    _F()
else
    if LogToConsole then
        LogToConsole("4[Bendex Proxy] ERROR: Failed to load encrypted script! " .. tostring(_err))
    end
end
